### 2026-08-07 07:22:47  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Extends Gains as Hormuz Tensions Resurface — Brent rose above $83 per barrel on Friday, building on the previous session’s gains as renewed tensions in the Strait of Hormuz unsettled markets and cast fresh doubt over efforts to fully reopen the vital shipping route. Reports indicated that Iran struck what it described as “hostile targets” in the strait after explosions were reported near Qeshm Island. Under the proposed Iran-Oman agreement governing the waterway, Tehran seeks to prohibit US and Israeli vessels from transiting Hormuz and require countries deemed hostile to pay compensation before being granted passage. Iran has also proposed penalties equal to 20% of a vessel’s cargo value for violations and said the strait would only be fully reopened once the US maritime blockade is lifted. The Iranian parliament is currently reviewing the draft proposal, which outlines stricter conditions for commercial shipping through the Strait of Hormuz than markets anticipated.

【搶銅潮擾動全球供應鏈 AI基建擴張之下算力金屬供應承壓】AI算力基建的“狂飆”，讓算力金屬供給側緊張程度攀升。涵蓋銅、鎢、錫、鉭等相關的有色金屬品種被市場稱為“算力金屬”。8月6日，受剛果（金）禁止銅精礦出口影響，全球銅價再次重新整理歷史高點。與此同時，國內外AI企業繼續加碼資本開支，加大AI基礎設施投入。中輝期貨研究員肖豔麗認為，市場存在AI銅需求的時間錯配風險。從礦山勘探到投產平均需要15~18年，而AI資料中心的建設週期僅為2~3年。供給側15年以上的長週期與需求側2~3年的短週期之間形成了巨大的時間差，供需剪刀差在2028年前後可能達到極致，屆時銅價面臨結構性上行壓力。（證券時報）

### 2026-08-07 07:20:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #News #Importance 1

Trump to Impose 15% Tariffs on Polysilicon — The Trump administration on Thursday imposed a series of price floors and a 15% tariff on products made from polysilicon, the raw material used in semiconductors and solar panels. The proclamation, issued by U.S. President Donald Trump under Section 232 of the Trade Expansion Act of 1962, aims to protect U.S. polysilicon factories from growing Chinese ambitions in the chip supply chain. According to a White House document, the president set minimum import prices of $21 per kilogram for polysilicon, $100 per kilogram for polysilicon ingots and wafers, $0.22 per watt for solar cells, and $0.38 per watt for solar modules, or panels, as Reuters reported. The proclamation also authorizes the Commerce Department to create an incentive program for companies that invest in factories to produce polysilicon or derivative products.

NZX 50 Slips but Eyes Weekly Gain — New Zealand's stocks fell 94 points, or 0.7%, to 13,864 in Friday morning deals, extending losses from the previous session, tracking a decline on Wall Street overnight amid rising oil prices, which reinforced expectations that the Fed could raise interest rates at its upcoming meeting. Traders were cautious ahead of the release of China's trade data and US jobs data later today, as well as China's inflation data due on Sunday. Rising oil prices also pressured sentiment as concerns over inflation raised expectations that the RBNZ will hike interest rates. Energy, healthcare, consumer staples, and industrials mainly weighed on the index, with the early decliners including Auckland International Airport (-2.1%), A2 Milk (-2.1%), Fisher & Paykel (-2.1%), Channel Infrastructure (-0.9%), and Smart Healthcare (-0.8%). However, the index is heading for a 1.2% weekly rise after declining in the previous week.

【銀行保險資管行業三季度信心調查顯示：權益投資信心環比回升，受訪機構配置意願增強】近期，中國銀行保險資產管理業協會對198家機構（32家銀行理財公司、39家保險資管公司，以及127家保險集團、保險公司）進行調查，形成2026年三季度銀行保險資產管理行業投資信心指數。調查結果顯示，三季度銀行保險資產管理行業權益投資信心指數較二季度有所回升，權益層面風險偏好與配置意願同步回升，結構性機會共識增強。中國銀行保險資產管理業協會表示，銀行保險資管機構整體配置策略保持穩健，多數以維持現有倉位為基礎，透過小幅結構性調整最佳化組合。不同型別機構的配置偏好呈現差異化特徵。其中：受訪保險機構下半年看好股票、債券、證券投資基金三類資產；受訪銀行理財機構則看好“固收+”類資產。（上證報）

【特朗普宣佈對多晶矽及其衍生產品加徵關稅】當地時間8月6日，美國總統特朗普簽署行政令，依據《1962年貿易擴充套件法》第232條，對進口多晶矽及其衍生產品採取最低進口價格和額外關稅措施，以支援美國國內多晶矽、半導體和太陽能供應鏈。公告規定，最低進口價格分別為：多晶矽每公斤21美元；多晶矽錠和晶圓每公斤100美元；太陽能電池每瓦0.22美元；太陽能元件每瓦0.38美元。同時，美國將對公告附件所列多晶矽錠及相關衍生產品加徵15%的從價關稅。相關措施將自2026年12月4日美國東部時間凌晨12時01分起生效。公告還授權商務部建立“迴流美國”激勵計劃。企業如承諾在美國建設、翻新或擴建多晶矽、矽錠、晶圓或太陽能電池生產設施，並在2029年1月20日前開工，可申請部分進口裝置和相關產品免繳第232條關稅。（央視新聞）

### 2026-08-07 05:03:38  #Trading Economics #Markets #Mexico #Currency #Importance 1

Mexican Peso Gains After Banxico Meeting — The Mexican peso strengthened to 17.21 per USD from the near four-month low of 17.60 reached on July 24th after Banxico kept its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 6.50% and signaled that it expects to maintain the current policy stance. The central bank held rates steady for a second consecutive meeting as policymakers balanced falling headline inflation and weaker economic activity against persistent services inflation and risks from the Middle East conflict. Banxico reiterated that future decisions will depend on incoming data and highlighted uncertainty surrounding geopolitical tensions and global trade policies. The peso continued to benefit from a wide interest rate differential, with Mexico's policy rate at 6.50% compared with the Federal Reserve's 3.50%-3.75% target range.

### 2026-08-07 04:51:54  #國際

【特朗普簽署針對出生公民權的行政令 將嚴厲打擊“生育旅遊”】當地時間8月6日，美國總統特朗普在白宮簽署兩項行政令，重點打擊商業化“生育旅遊”，並進一步限制部分在美國出生兒童自動獲得公民身份的範圍。
特朗普表示，政府將對透過酒店等機構組織赴美生子的“生育旅遊”產業展開更大規模、更強力的執法行動。
特朗普稱，出生公民權制度長期被濫用，“生育旅遊”已經發展成一門生意。他聲稱，每年可能有“數十萬人”透過相關方式讓子女取得美國公民身份，並舉例稱，有人曾以家庭名義攜帶數十名兒童參與相關安排。
特朗普還表示，此前最高法院對其限制出生公民權的政策作出“不公平”裁決，因此政府正透過新的方式推進限制措施。他認為最新行政令符合憲法，並稱政府將結束利用商業化“生育旅遊”獲取美國公民身份的做法。
這是特朗普第二次試圖收緊出生公民權，預計仍將面臨憲法層面的法律挑戰。美國最高法院今年6月30日曾否決特朗普此前更廣泛限制非公民子女出生公民權的行政令。（央視新聞）

Colombia Producer Inflation Eases in July — Colombia's Producer Price Index rose 1.51% year-on-year in July 2026, slowing from 3.30% in June and marking the weakest annual increase since February. Among the main sectors, agriculture, livestock, and fishing recorded the strongest annual gain at 8.69%, while manufacturing prices rose 0.44%. In contrast, mining prices fell 4.77%. The largest positive contributions to annual producer inflation came from pome and stone fruits (29.73%), citrus fruits (76.62%), and potatoes (93.51%). Meanwhile, the biggest downward contributions came from crude petroleum and oils obtained from bituminous minerals (-7.65%), milled or green coffee (-20.17%), and coffee (-12.97%).

Ibovespa Slides as BCB Signals Caution — The Ibovespa fell 1.2% to close at 175,546 on Thursday after the BCB signaled a cautious approach to further interest rate cuts. The Copom lowered the Selic rate by 25 basis points to 14.0% on Wednesday but stopped short of signaling another cut in September, instead reiterating that future decisions will depend on incoming data. Bond yields rose as the statement emphasized the need to maintain an appropriate degree of monetary restraint to ensure inflation converges to target and highlighted elevated global uncertainty. Financial stocks posted losses, with Itaú down 1.3%, Banco do Brasil retreating 3.7%. Bradesco fell 1.9% after reporting weaker credit quality indicators. Axia erased earlier gains to close down 1% despite reporting a rebound in net profit. Major exporters also declined after Brazil posted a weaker-than-expected trade surplus, with Vale down 1.7%, Gerdau and CSN losing 2.5%, Embraer falling 1.5%, and Minerva shedding 3%.

### 2026-08-07 03:27:40  #Trading Economics #Economy #Mexico #Interest Rate #Importance 2

Central Bank of Mexico Keeps Benchmark Rate Unchanged — Mexico's central bank left its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 6.50% at its August meeting, in line with market expectations. Policymakers said the decision reflects an appropriate degree of monetary restraint as inflation continues to ease while risks remain tilted to the upside. Annual inflation slowed to 3.10% in the first half of July from 3.55% in mid-June, while core inflation eased to 3.95%. The central bank noted that inflation is expected to continue declining gradually, with headline inflation projected to converge to the 3% target in the fourth quarter of 2027. Banxico also highlighted that Mexico's economy rebounded in the second quarter after contracting in the previous quarter, although downside risks to growth persist. The board reiterated that it expects to keep the benchmark rate at its current level, citing uncertainty related to geopolitical conflicts, global trade policies, and their potential impact on inflation.

Brazil Trade Surplus Misses Forecasts — Brazil's trade surplus widened 1.0% year-on-year to $7.07 billion in July 2026, below market expectations of an $8.4 billion surplus. Exports rose 6.2% to $34.12 billion. Agricultural exports increased 9.3%, led by live animals (29.9%), soybeans (17.4%), and raw cotton (27.2%). Shipments from extractive industries advanced 10.8%, supported by other crude minerals (55.5%), base metal ores and concentrates (101.5%), and crude petroleum (23.8%). Manufacturing exports rose 2.4%, boosted by pulp (30.8%), fuel oils excluding crude (63.0%), and vegetable fats and oils (149.5%). Meanwhile, imports climbed 7.6% to $27.05 billion, driven by a 31.4% increase in extractive industry purchases and a 6.9% rise in manufacturing imports, led by pharmaceuticals (44.1%), fuel oils (31.4%), and data processing equipment (216.7%).

Brent Rises More Than 4% — Brent extended its gains, rising more than 4% to $82.8 per barrel on Thursday, after reports that an Iranian parliamentary committee is analyzing a draft proposal outlining restrictive conditions for ship traffic through the Strait of Hormuz. Under the proposal, Iran would prohibit US and Israeli vessels from transiting Hormuz and require countries deemed hostile to pay compensation before being granted passage. Tehran also proposed imposing penalties on violators equivalent to 20% of the value of the cargo carried by a vessel and stated that a full reopening of the strait would depend on the lifting of the US maritime blockade. Meanwhile, Houthi forces in Yemen claimed on Thursday to have launched attacks on Saudi troop positions, adding to geopolitical concerns across the region. Earlier in the day, US officials reiterated their confidence that a nuclear agreement with Iran was within reach, although investors remained cautious about the prospects for a durable and lasting peace.

### 2026-08-07 03:00:59  #其他

【塔隆當選貝南新設參議院首任議長】波多諾伏訊息：貝南前總統帕特里斯·塔隆6日當選該國新設參議院的首任議長。 參議院議長選舉當天在貝南首都波多諾伏舉行。25名參議員投票，塔隆以24票贊成、0票反對、1票棄權當選議長，任期5年。國民議會前議長路易·弗拉沃努當選副議長。此次選舉標誌著貝南參議院組建工作完成。 塔隆在當選後表示，將致力於“加強國家團結，維護國家政治穩定、和平與民主”。 現年68歲的塔隆2016年4月就任貝南總統，2021年4月獲得連任，2026年5月卸任。 根據2025年12月17日頒佈的憲法修正案，貝南議會由原來的單一國民議會改為由國民議會和參議院組成的兩院制。首屆參議院7月30日正式成立。 根據貝南憲法，參議院負責維護國家統一，促進國家建設、政治穩定、民主與和平，維護政治休戰，並可在憲法規定條件下，對有關政治行為主體作出暫停或剝奪政治權利、公民權利等決定。（新華社）

US Stocks Pull Back — US stocks swung lower on Thursday as a rebound in energy prices rekindled worries of a Federal Reserve rate hike next month. The S&P 500 inched down, the Dow lost 0.7%, and the Nasdaq 100 fell 0.4%. Yields rebounded together with wholesale fuel prices as markets reassessed the likelihood of tanker flows being restored in the Strait of Hormuz. On top of that, a series of hawkish reaction functions claimed by FOMC members were amplified by a report stating Fed Chairman Warsh is willing to hike rates should inflation rise further. Financial stocks edged down with Citi, Wells Fargo, and Amex dropping more than 1%, while credit-sensitive hyperscalers were mostly lower with Alphabet dropping 1%. Meanwhile, Western Digital plummeted 12% after posting a disappointing guidance. Similarly, Sandisk lost nearly 6% as its results failed to meet lofty expectations for memory producers. SpaceX fell rebounded to a 2% gain as $101 billion in its shares are detached from their post-IPO lockup period.

Lumber Nears 3-Month Low — Lumber prices fell below $580 per thousand board feet, heading toward a three-month low as weaker US housing demand and persistent trade uncertainty weighed on the market. While Canadian softwood lumber was exempt from US's new 50% tariffs, it remains subject to existing duties of nearly 35%, pressuring producers' margins. The Forest Products Association of Canada said the trade measures dealt a further blow to the industry already operating below capacity amid production curtailments and closures, while Ottawa has extended support measures. The US Lumber Coalition argued that Canada's excess production capacity continues to distort the market. Meanwhile, mortgage rates climbed to their highest level in a year, according to Freddie Mac, while mortgage applications declined in the final weeks of July, reflecting deteriorating homebuying activity. Demand was further pressured by weaker construction spending on lumber-intensive single-family housing, falling 3.3% from last year in June.

US 30-Year Mortgage Rate Rises to 1-Year High — The average rate on a 30-year fixed mortgage in the US rose to 6.69% as of August 8, 2026, from 6.66% a week earlier, marking a fifth consecutive weekly increase and the highest level since late July 2025. Mortgage rates continued to climb as the war with Iran and the Federal Reserve's decision to keep interest rates unchanged fueled inflation concerns. The rise in borrowing costs has further strained affordability, keeping many prospective homebuyers on the sidelines. As a result, applications for new mortgages declined in the final two weeks of July, according to the Mortgage Bankers Association. Still, “While mortgage rates continue to influence affordability, the housing market is showing signs of adjustment, with listing prices modestly below year-ago levels and for-sale inventory improving from the limited supply seen in recent years.”, said Sam Khater, Freddie Mac's Chief Economist.

【各期限美債收益率上揚 油價上漲推升加息預期 且Alphabet大舉發債】美國國債下跌。油價從三週低點反彈，推動市場重新押注美聯儲加息；與此同時，谷歌母公司Alphabet Inc.計劃進行一筆鉅額公司債發行。各期限美債收益率上升2至4個基點，其中短期收益率漲幅最大。油價上漲已推動加息預期升溫，而媒體報道稱，如果未來幾周公布的通脹資料偏熱，美聯儲主席凱文·沃什準備加息，進一步強化了這一預期。道明證券美國利率策略師Molly Brooks表示：“美聯儲或許確實需要真正加息，才能證明其認真對待抗擊通脹。”美國下週將公佈7月消費者價格指數和生產者價格指數等關鍵通脹資料，7月就業報告則定於本週五釋出。美聯儲的雙重使命包括維持物價穩定和實現充分就業，因此勞動力市場疲軟也可能限制其為壓低通脹而加息的意願。在Alphabet宣佈將發行10部分債券以籌集高達250億美元資金後，較長期國債收益率觸及盤中高點。

### 2026-08-06 23:55:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 0

South Africa 10-Year Bond Yield Inches Up — South Africa's 10-year government bond yield rose to around 8.55%, up from a near four-week low of 8.47% hit on August 5, as traders assessed the chances of a potential US-Iran agreement and its implications on inflation and interest rates. Markets remain skeptical that the US and Iran will reach a lasting deal soon to reopen the Strait of Hormuz, keeping upward pressure on energy prices and reinforcing inflation concerns. Domestically, the South African Reserve Bank (SARB) kept its repo rate at 7.00% last month, citing weak economic growth while acknowledging persistent inflation risks that leave the door open to a 25-basis-point hike later this year. The annual inflation accelerated to 5.0% in June, the highest in two years, from 4.5% in May, driven mainly by higher fuel costs. The central bank expects headline inflation to remain above 4.0% until early next year, staying above its 3.0% target

### 2026-08-06 23:48:35  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Brazil 10-Year Bond Yield Rises After BCB Meeting — Brazil's 10-year government bond yield rose to 14.52% from the three-week low of 14.42% reached on August 4th after the Copom signaled a cautious approach to further interest rate cuts. Although the BCB lowered the Selic rate by a widely expected 25 basis points to 14.0%, its lowest level since March 2025, policymakers stressed that the pace and extent of further easing will depend on incoming economic data and the inflation outlook. The committee also cited heightened uncertainty stemming from conflicts in the Middle East and from the monetary policy outlook in advanced economies. Meanwhile, Brazil created a net 145,161 formal jobs in June, well above expectations of 115,000, reinforcing labor market resilience and supporting expectations that interest rates will remain restrictive. Fiscal concerns also persisted amid signals of higher government spending and pressure on revenues from new US tariffs.

### 2026-08-06 23:42:46  #Trading Economics #Markets #United Kingdom #Stock Market #Importance 1

FTSE 100 Falls on Thursday — The FTSE 100 erased early gains to close marginally lower on Thursday, as a wave of heavyweight stocks trading ex-dividend offset broader market optimism over progress toward reopening the Strait of Hormuz. Lloyds, Barclays, Relx and Reckitt Benckiser all declined after losing entitlement to their latest dividend payments, while other defensive heavyweights including AstraZeneca and Unilever also weighed on the index. Relx was among the worst performers, falling more than 4%. Tritax Big Box also lost around 4% after raising £350 million through a share placing to finance its data centre expansion, a move analysts viewed as dilutive in the near term. On the upside, Diageo surged more than 5.5% after unveiling a restructuring plan, forecasting stable sales this year and announcing $1 billion in cost savings over the next three years. Persimmon gained more than 3% after reporting stronger-than-expected earnings and reaffirming the upper end of its annual home completion guidance.

Cocoa Futures Hover Near 1-Month High — Cocoa prices traded around $5,800 per tonne, close to their highest in nearly a month, influenced by crop forecasts and ongoing climate risks. Supply concerns have intensified in the key producing region of West Africa after Ghana's cocoa regulator COCOBOD projected a decline of at least 16% in 2026/27 cocoa production due to unfavorable weather, disease, ageing plantations, illegal mining, and the crop's natural yield cycle. Expectations of a more than 10% drop in Ivory Coast's output next season, along with the risk of excessive rainfall boosting disease pressure, further supported prices. Additionally, concerns mounted that Ecuador, Peru and Southeast Asia could together produce 100,000 tons less cocoa.

【內地零售物業表現突出，太古地產上半年股東應占基本溢利同比增加11%】伴隨著內地消費市場持續修復，太古地產（HK01972，股價23.00港元，市值1324億港元）旗下內地零售物業租金收入規模已超過香港寫字樓組合，成為這家香港老牌商業地產企業最強勁的收入支柱。8月6日，太古地產釋出的2026年中期業績公告（以下簡稱業績公告）顯示，今年上半年，公司實現收入94.13億港元，同比增長8%；股東應占基本溢利49億港元，同比增長11%，主要反映出售深水灣道6號兩座獨立屋所得溢利，惟部分被2025年上半年出售美國邁阿密Brickell City Centre商場及毗鄰土地的權益所確認的非經常性溢利抵銷；股東應占經常性基本溢利46.61億港元，同比增長36%。分業務來看，包括租金和屋苑管理費在內的物業投資業務，仍是太古地產的收入支柱。太古地產業績公告顯示，今年上半年，物業投資業務貢獻了66.93億港元的營收，物業買賣業務貢獻了22億港元的營收，酒店業務則是貢獻了5.2億港元的營收。太古地產這三大業務板塊在今年上半年均實現同比增長。（每經）

### 2026-08-06 22:47:14  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Arabica Coffee Futures Settle Below $3.1 — Arabica coffee futures have fluctuated in recent weeks, before consolidating below $3.10 per pound to their lowest level in a month. Still, the market continued to be supported by tight supplies, uncertainty over the quality of Brazil's harvest, coffee growers' reluctance to sell, and weather-related risks associated with El Niño. Although a record Brazilian harvest of more than 70 million bags is expected this season, the balance between supply and demand remains tight, particularly in the higher-quality. Traders remain concerned that rains in key producing regions, including Minas Gerais and São Paulo, during June and July, may have affected the quality of part of the crop. ICE arabica coffee fell to a 2.5-year low of 251,417 bags as of August 7, down sharply from the 754,516 bags last year. At the same time, Brazilian producers continue to sell gradually, limiting immediate coffee availability. Meanwhile, the developing "super" El Niño remains a key risk to next year's harvest.

【義大利首次對全部監測城市釋出高溫紅色預警】據義大利安莎社報道，受持續熱浪影響，義大利衛生部納入高溫監測的全國27座主要城市6日全部處於最高等級的紅色預警狀態。這是義大利首次對全部監測城市釋出高溫紅色預警。 義大利衛生部表示，紅色預警意味著持續的高溫緊急狀態，不僅會對老年人、慢性病患者等脆弱群體的健康構成威脅，也可能對健康人群造成影響。 義大利氣象部門預計，本輪高溫天氣仍將持續至少10天，部分地區最高氣溫將達到40攝氏度。義大利衛生部發布的預警資訊顯示，除義大利北部城市博爾扎諾外，其他主要城市預計7日仍將處於紅色預警狀態。 安莎社的報道說，義大利正處於今年夏季以來的第四輪大範圍熱浪。持續高溫已造成多人死亡。高溫疊加乾旱和野火對經濟活動造成衝擊，農業受到的影響尤為明顯。 世界自然基金會5日釋出的一份報告顯示，今年夏季以來，義大利野火的過火面積已達約7萬公頃。(新華社)

US Natural Gas Stocks Rise More Than Expected — US energy firms added 33 billion cubic feet of natural gas into storage in the week ending July 31, above market expectations of 31 bcf. In the prior week ended July 24, energy firms added 28 bcf of gas into storage.

US Wholesale Inventory Growth Revised Down — US wholesale inventories rose 0.2% month-over-month to $944.7 billion in June 2026, below a 0.3% gain in the advance estimate. The latest reading marked the fourth consecutive month of slower inventory growth after a 1.5% increase in March, the strongest since mid-2022. Compared with a year earlier, wholesale inventories were 4.2% higher. Meanwhile, the inventories-to-sales ratio for merchant wholesalers, excluding manufacturers’ sales branches and offices, stood at 1.19 on a seasonally adjusted basis, down from 1.30 in June 2025.

### 2026-08-06 22:14:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Services PMI #Importance 1

Canada Services PMI Remains in Contraction — Canada's S&P Global Services PMI rose to 49.1 in July 2026 from 47.1 in June but remained below the 50.0 threshold, signaling a second consecutive, albeit milder, contraction in the services sector. Business conditions continued to be weighed down by tariffs and geopolitical tensions, which dampened both current activity and the outlook. Output and new orders declined again, though at a slower pace, while business confidence fell to its lowest level of 2026. Meanwhile, input cost inflation remained elevated, driven by tariffs, higher energy and fuel costs linked to the Middle East conflict, and rising labor expenses. Firms continued to raise selling prices despite weak demand, highlighting persistent inflationary pressures and risks to the broader economic outlook.

### 2026-08-06 22:13:11  #公司 #國際

【特斯拉Terafab晶片工廠正式落戶得州 瞄準1太瓦算力自主供應】特斯拉正式宣佈其代號為“Terafab”的超大規模晶片製造專案將落戶美國得克薩斯州格萊姆斯縣。該專案由特斯拉、SpaceX及xAI聯合主導，旨在建設全球規模最大的半導體制造設施，以應對旗下公司在人工智慧、自動駕駛及太空任務領域日益增長的算力需求。erafab並非傳統意義上的代工廠，而是一座高度垂直整合的先進半導體晶圓廠。特斯拉明確表示，該專案將邏輯晶片、儲存晶片、先進封裝及測試環節整合於同一設施內，製造面積規劃超過1億平方英尺。這種全流程閉環設計旨在實現晶片從設計到量產的快速迭代，加速新計算能力的部署。在產能規劃上，Terafab設定了極具雄心的目標：年產能超過1太瓦（TW）計算能力。這一規模約為當前全球晶片年產能的50倍，遠超現有供應商的供應增速。特斯拉指出，儘管現有晶片供應商在擴大產能，但其擴張速度仍無法滿足特斯拉與SpaceX在機器人、自動駕駛及AI資料中心領域的指數級需求增長。

### 2026-08-06 22:12:03  #MKT News #Market Themes #Chip Wars #Magnificent 7 #Star Stocks #Tesla #TSLA.O

【Tesla: Terafab to build Grimes County, Texas wafer fab targeting >1 TW compute capacity】Tesla and SpaceX will locate Terafab in Grimes County, Texas. The project is a vertically integrated advanced wafer fab covering advanced logic, memory, packaging and test, with planned manufacturing area of more than 100 million sq ft. Tesla and SpaceX forecast combined chip demand exceeding 1 terawatt (TW) of compute, a scale they say outstrips current global supply. Terafab is intended to close the widening supply-demand gap and speed deployment by co-locating manufacturing, packaging and testing. Tesla began construction in April on an R&D wafer fab at its Texas Gigafactory north campus as a precursor and urged existing chip suppliers to expand capacity where feasible.

Baltic Dry Index Breaks 5-Day Advance — The Baltic Exchange's dry bulk freight index, which monitors rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, snapped a five-day winning streak on Thursday, falling about 0.2% to 3,057 points. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes including iron ore and coal, also halted its five-day advance, decreasing by 0.8% to 5,052 points; and the supramax index shed 4 points, or 0.3%, at 1,608 points. 【天文望遠鏡觀測到“獵鷹9”火箭殘骸撞擊月球跡象】歐洲南方天文臺表示，其位於智利的甚大望遠鏡觀測到美國太空探索技術公司廢棄的一枚“獵鷹9”號火箭殘骸在美國東部時間5日凌晨撞擊月球的相關跡象。據路透社報道，撞擊發生於美國東部時間5日2時35分（北京時間5日14時35分）左右，當時繞月飛行的少數幾個航天器均未能拍攝到撞擊的近距離畫面，但歐洲南方天文臺的甚大望遠鏡捕捉到了撞擊產生的微弱光跡。歐洲南方天文臺一名發言人告訴路透社：“我們可以確認，在撞擊發生後，望遠鏡探測到了撞擊羽流中鈉和鋰氣體的光譜線，持續時間約為5至10分鐘。”美聯社援引美國波士頓大學天文學家卡爾·施密特的話說，他“100%確定”其團隊觀測到了撞擊發生的跡象，“我們沒有撞擊的影象，但有望遠鏡證據”。（新華社）

Canada Private Sector Activity Contracts for 2nd Month — The S&P Global Canada Composite PMI rose to 49.7 in July 2026 from 47.9 in June, signaling a second consecutive month of contraction in private-sector business activity, albeit at a slower pace. The improvement reflected stronger manufacturing output, while the services sector remained in contraction. New business followed a similar pattern. Employment continued to expand across the private sector, though business confidence weakened to its lowest level since June 2025. On the price front, both input costs and output charges increased at faster rates.

Dow Holds Record High, Nasdaq Falls — US stocks were mixed on Thursday as stability for traditional economic sectors weighed against volatility for the AI trade following fresh earnings results. The S&P 500 was flat for a second session, while the Dow inched higher for another record high. Treasury yields held the week's pullback, moving in tandem with energy prices to reflect softer concerns that the Federal Reserve will raise inters rates this year. Some relief in the macroeconomic backdrop coincided with robust earnings, with Eli Lilly holding yesterday's results-driven surge while J&J and Goldman Sachs gained around 1%. In turn, chip producers were sharply down to extend their volatile momentum, pressuring the Nasdaq 100 to a near 1% pullback. Western Digital plummeted 17% after posting a disappointing guidance. Similarly, Sandisk lost nearly 10% as its results failed to meet lofty expectations for memory producers. SpaceX fell up to 1% as $101 billion in its shares are will be free from their post-IPO lockup period.

Dollar Holds Near Lows Before Payrolls Report — The dollar index edged up to 99.8 on Thursday but remained close to a seven-week low as investors balanced optimism over developments in the Middle East with caution ahead of Friday's closely watched US jobs report. Iran said it had reached an agreement with Oman on a proposed shipping route through the Strait of Hormuz, raising hopes that energy flows through the critical waterway could resume. However, a broader US-Iran agreement remains uncertain, with President Donald Trump saying negotiations were ongoing and that he would “see what happens.” Attention has also shifted to the upcoming nonfarm payrolls report, which is expected to provide fresh insight into the strength of the US labor market and the Federal Reserve's policy outlook. A stronger-than-expected employment reading would likely reinforce expectations of further interest rate hikes, while a weaker report could prompt markets to scale back tightening bets and reduce upward pressure on Treasury yields.

### 2026-08-06 21:14:09  #Trading Economics #Markets #Brazil #Currency #Importance 1

Brazilian Real Gains After BCB Signals Caution — The Brazilian real strengthened to 5.11 per USD from the three-week low of 5.145 reached on August 4th after the Copom signaled a cautious approach to further rate cuts. The BCB lowered the Selic rate by a widely expected 25 basis points to 14.0%, its lowest level since March 2025. However, policymakers stressed that the pace and extent of further easing will depend on incoming economic data and the inflation outlook. The committee also highlighted heightened global uncertainty stemming from conflicts in the Middle East and uncertainty over monetary policy in advanced economies, calling for caution among emerging markets. The decision came one week after the Federal Reserve left its benchmark rate unchanged at a target 3.50%-3.75%, preserving a wide interest rate differential that continues to support the real.

【World Gold Council: High real rates have not suppressed gold; central-bank buying and Asian demand support pricing】World Gold Council says central-bank purchases and Asian investor demand are becoming increasingly important and may not move in step with US rates, the dollar or inflation. That does not imply real rates have lost influence; in the near term rising yields can still pressure gold. But if tight policy eventually triggers a growth shock, rising inflation or stress in part of the financial system, longer-term yields could fall and, combined with central-bank and Asian demand, provide support for gold — though not necessarily a repeat of 2025’s outsized gains.

### 2026-08-06 21:07:17  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 2

Copper Rises to Record High — Copper futures rose to over $6.7 per pound on Thursday, a record high, amid mounting concerns of tight supply and signs of robust demand. The DR Congo announced a ban on exports of copper concentrate effective immediately. The move was in line with growth protectionism for ore-rich countries to spur the development of refining and processing capacity domestically. Meanwhile, development at a portion of Codelco's flagship El Teniente mine could remain suspended for as long as two years. Meanwhile, trading funds continued to take long positions on metal exchanges amid scarcity for on-warrant inventories, falling visible stockpiles in China, and robust US buying ahead of a possible tariff announcement. Data showed that more than 200,000 tons of copper arrived at US ports in July, marking the largest monthly inflow in over a decade. Likewise, deliverable copper stocks an SHFE inventories nearly halved in July to 69.3 thousand tonnes.

【U.S. Q2 labor productivity accelerates; corporate cost-cutting evident】U.S. nonfarm labor productivity rose at a 1.4% annualized rate in Q2, above a revised 0.8% in Q1 and beating consensus. Unit labor costs rose 1.3%, below expectations. Fed officials, investors and economists are monitoring whether large-scale AI investment is lifting productivity, but official quarterly data are volatile and a clear trend will take time to emerge. Labor costs remain a major corporate expense; efficiency gains can allow wage increases without adding inflationary pressure. Some economists warn sustained AI-driven productivity gains could prompt firms to delay hiring or cut staff. Q2 productivity was supported by the strongest output growth since Q3 2025 while hours worked rose only modestly.

### 2026-08-06 21:00:47  #資料 #市場 #央行 #國際

【美國二季度勞動生產率加速增長 企業降本成效顯現】美國第二季度勞動生產率增速快於預期，主要原因是企業尋求緩解成本上升帶來的壓力。週四公佈的資料顯示，二季度非農生產力按年率計算增長1.4%，高於第一季度經上修後0.8%的增幅，這一結果亦超過了市場的普遍預測。而單位勞動力成本則上漲1.3%，低於預期。美聯儲官員、投資者和經濟學家一直在尋找跡象，以判斷數千億美元的人工智慧投資是否正在提升勞動生產率。不過，由於官方資料季度間波動較大，要觀察到清晰趨勢仍需要時間。勞動力成本是許多企業最大的支出專案之一，而效率提升能夠讓工資上漲而不會推高通脹。長期來看，提高生產率有助於改善生活水平，但一些經濟學家擔心，如果AI帶來的生產率增長持續存在，部分企業可能會推遲招聘，甚至削減員工。第二季度生產率增長得益於產出實現自2025年第三季度以來最強勁增長，同時員工工作時長增幅較為溫和。

【世界黃金協會：年初至今全球黃金ETF累計流入110億美元】世界黃金協會報告顯示，全球黃金掛鉤 ETF 7 月錄得 30 億美元資金流入，終結連續兩個月資金流出態勢。資金流入覆蓋全部地區，歐洲上市基金領漲。正向資金疊加金價走高，推動全球黃金 ETF 資產管理規模上漲 1% 至 5300 億美元，總持倉增加 23 噸至 4068 噸，但仍低於 2026 年 2 月 27 日創下的 4176 噸歷史最高持倉。年初至今，全球黃金 ETF 累計流入 110 億美元，對應持倉增加 39 噸。亞洲上市基金是這一階段全球資金流入的最大來源，歐洲緊隨其後；而北美依舊維持資金淨流出狀態。7 月全球黃金 ETF 重回資金流入，一方面源於投資者借金價低位選擇性重新入場，另一方面來自資產組合多元化配置的持續需求。歐洲投資者在 6 月拋售過後重新建立黃金倉位，抓住金價回落視窗重返市場。該走勢與今年早些時候類似：3 月美國主導的大規模贖回後，歐洲基金率先帶動市場反彈，說明市場下跌後投資者願意增持黃金敞口。亞洲 7 月黃金 ETF 資金流入 6.16 億美元，鞏固其作為年初至今全球最大資金流入地區的地位。

【高盛大幅上調AI伺服器PCB和CCL預測 2028年市場規模將達840億和480億美元】高盛在最新發布的全球印刷電路板（PCB）和覆銅板（CCL）行業報告中，大幅上調人工智慧（AI）伺服器相關市場規模預測。該行預計，全球AI伺服器PCB市場將在2027年達到375億美元，較此前預測上調38%，2028年進一步增至840億美元；CCL市場2027年預計達到221億美元，上調18%，2028年則有望增至480億美元。這意味著，2026年至2028年，AI伺服器PCB和CCL市場規模的複合年增長率將分別達到148%和161%。與傳統伺服器週期不同，本輪增長不僅來自伺服器出貨量增加，還受到PCB層數提高、高階HDI（高密度互連）滲透、CCL材料升級，以及整機櫃內部PCB使用量增加等多重因素推動。

Czech National Bank Leaves Key Rate Unchanged — The Czech National Bank left its two-week repo rate unchanged at 3.75% in August 2026, pausing after delivering its first interest rate hike since 2022 at the previous meeting. Although headline inflation remained below the central bank's target for most of the year, policymakers said demand-driven inflationary pressures persisted. The bank noted that headline inflation was held down by volatile components, particularly food prices, while government measures to contain price growth also helped keep inflation below target. Meanwhile, economic growth slowed in the second quarter of 2026 to 2% from 2.2%.

US Q1 Productivity Rises More than Expected — US nonfarm business sector labor productivity rose 1.4% in the second quarter of 2026, more than market expectations of a 0.6% increase, preliminary estimates showed. Output increased 1.7 percent and hours worked increased 0.3 percent. Meanwhile, manufacturing sector labor productivity increased 1.9% in the second quarter of 2026, as output increased 4.6 percent and hours worked increased 2.6 percent. In the durable manufacturing sector, productivity increased 2.7% and in the nondurable manufacturing sector productivity increased 2%. From the same quarter a year ago, nonfarm business sector labor productivity increased 2.2% in the second quarter of 2026. Total manufacturing sector productivity increased 0.9% from the same quarter a year ago.

US Initial Jobless Claims Rise Less than Expected — The number of people claiming unemployment benefits in the US inched higher by 1,000 to 199,000 on the final week of July, slightly under market expectations of 202,000 to remain near the 57-year low of 188,000 two weeks prior. Continuing claims, which are seen as a gauge of outstanding unemployment in the US, rose by 24,000 to 1,801,000 in the earlier week, remaining well under averages from this year. The data continued to reflect a strong labor market in the US, aligned with statements from FOMC members that see the US economy in full employment. Meanwhile, initial claims filed by federal employees, which have been under scrutiny due the administration's efforts in decreasing the number of public workers, rose by 32 to 450.

Ghana Inflation Rate Slows to 4.6% in July — The annual inflation rate in Ghana fell to 4.6% in July 2026 from a six-month high of 5.3% in the prior month. This marked the first monthly decline after three consecutive increases. Food inflation slowed to 3.1% from 3.9% the month before, as a more stable exchange rate helped curb imported inflation. The cost of imported goods rose by 2% year-on-year in July, slowing from a 2.3% rise in June. On a monthly basis, the CPI edged up by 0.1% in July, after a 0.2% increase in the previous month.

Gold Holds at 7-Week High — Gold prices were above $4,250 per ounce on Thursday, holding this week's rally at a seven week high as lower energy prices reduced certainty that the Federal Reserve was due to raise rates this year. US officials signaled they are chasing a ceasefire with Iran and an agreement to protect trade through the Persian Gulf, driving fuel prices to plunge since the start of August, and softening concerns that energy inflation would lift underlying consumer prices. Treasury yields consequently eased, lifting bullion prices as markets faced lower opportunity costs to hold assets that bear no interest. Elsewhere, data from clearing institutions indicated that institutional investors in China continued to increase long position on gold-backed assets for safety from volatility in tech stocks and recent signs of strength in the physical market, recently underpinned by central banks.

### 2026-08-06 19:44:32  #其他

【多瑙河流量降至40多年來最低 羅馬尼亞能源供應承壓】受持續高溫乾旱影響，多瑙河水位和流量持續下降。當地時間8月6日，羅馬尼亞水務部門資料顯示，多瑙河在羅馬尼亞入境河段流量約為每秒1400立方米，降至1985年以來的最低水平，僅約為往年8月平均流量的三分之一。多瑙河水流量持續下降給羅馬尼亞能源供應帶來壓力。位於多瑙河畔的切爾納沃德核電站兩座反應堆依靠河水進行冷卻，由於水位過低，羅方已於當地時間7月28日起停運該核電站的1號反應堆。為儘可能延長2號反應堆執行時間，羅馬尼亞有關部門計劃將4艘裝載岩石的駁船沉入河中，形成臨時阻擋結構，將更多河水引向核電站取水區域。核電站方面表示，如果水位繼續下降且相關工程措施無法發揮作用，另一座反應堆可能在未來五至六天內停運。該核電站為羅馬尼亞唯一核電站，其兩座核反應堆原本供應該國大約20%電力。與此同時，羅馬尼亞能源部呼籲居民、企業和公共機構在8月31日前自願減少用電，以緩解電力供應壓力。羅馬尼亞能源部表示，受嚴重乾旱和多瑙河流量大幅下降影響，羅馬尼亞目前正面臨能源供應壓力。除核電受到影響外，水電供應同樣受到低水位制約。由於鄰國部分地區也面臨類似情況，電力進口也受到限制。（央視新聞）

### 2026-08-06 19:43:37  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution

【Cheap models don't guarantee cheap tasks — AI costs need recalculation】Per Artificial Analysis, DeepSeek V4 Flash scored 50 on its intelligence index, ranking 3rd of 101 models and about twice the median among open-weight models. Output speed is ~104 tokens/sec; input and output pricing are $0.14 and $0.28 per mln tokens. The report says competition is shifting from peak performance to cost-per-completed-task. Low price, open weights and mln-token context position DeepSeek for batch search, coding assistance and multi-agent calls, and will pressure high-priced closed models to defend premiums via reliability, ecosystem and enterprise services. Caveats: DeepSeek generated ~210 mln tokens during the review—roughly double the peer median—suggesting verbose inference, and benchmarks do not fully capture complex-task success rates; a low token price may not translate into proportional cost savings for all real workloads.

US 10-Year Treasury Yield Edges Up — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note edged higher to 4.63% on Thursday as investors continued to assess the outlook for monetary policy and the path of interest rates. Markets see around a 55% chance of a Fed rate hike at its September meeting, down from 68% on Monday. Attention now turns to Friday's US jobs report, which is expected to provide fresh insight into labour market conditions. Investors expect payroll growth to remain resilient, although ADP data released earlier this week showed private-sector hiring slowed sharply in July. Meanwhile, announced job cuts fell to their lowest level in two years. At the same time, geopolitical risks remain elevated. A lasting agreement between the US and Iran appears unlikely in the short term, keeping pressure on energy prices and sustaining concerns that higher commodity costs would fuel inflation, pressuring the Fed to raise borrowing costs.

【SpaceX火箭殘骸撞月，激起塵埃雲】據路透社8月5日報道，美國太空探索技術公司（SpaceX）一塊自去年起在太空中漂浮的火箭殘骸5日凌晨高速撞擊月球。這塊校車大小的殘骸是一枚SpaceX“獵鷹9”號火箭的上面級，它曾於2025年1月把美國“螢火蟲”航空航天公司的月球著陸器送往月球。該火箭殘骸與月球的預期碰撞成為天文學家和航天愛好者的一大看點，他們熱切期待目睹此次撞擊的發生過程。重約4噸的火箭箭體在美國東部時間凌晨2時35分左右以8690千米的時速撞擊月球。環繞月球飛行的幾臺航天器均處在無法拍攝到撞擊特寫畫面的位置。不過，架設在智利帕拉納爾天文臺的歐洲南方天文臺甚大望遠鏡捕捉到了此次撞擊所產生的微弱閃光。歐洲南方天文臺發言人對路透社表示：“我們可以確認，望遠鏡探測到了撞擊後持續5至10分鐘的撞擊塵埃雲中鈉和鋰氣體的光譜線。”（參考訊息）

【Mainland nine cities in Guangdong‑Hong Kong‑Macao Greater Bay Area electricity use hits record in 1H 2026】Southern Power Grid says electricity consumption across the nine mainland cities of the Guangdong‑Hong Kong‑Macao Greater Bay Area reached 304.4 bln kWh in 1H 2026, up 8.1% YoY and a period record. Growth concentrated in high‑end manufacturing, digital compute and resilient external trade. Industrial power mix shows a sharp divergence: high‑tech equipment manufacturing electricity rose 8.6% YoY while energy‑intensive sectors fell 4.8%, signalling an accelerated shift from old to new growth drivers. High‑end manufacturing—notably autos, specialised equipment and electronics—now underpins industrial demand. In Guangzhou, auto sector power use increased as NEV output jumped 53.2% YoY in Jan–Jun, lifting lithium‑battery and smart‑vehicle electronics value added by 53.9% and 40.6% respectively, making the new‑energy chain a stabiliser for industrial activity.

US Stocks Reignite Diverging Trend — US futures were mixed on Thursday, as stability for traditional economic sectors weighed against volatility for the AI trade following fresh earrings results. In turn, chip producers were sharply down to extend their volatile momentum, pressuring the Nasdaq 100 to a near 1% pullback. Western Digital plummeted 17% after posting a disappointing guidance. 【廣西壯族自治區黨委召開2026年度發展壯大智慧經濟專題協商座談會 陳剛主持並講話】8月6日，自治區黨委在南寧召開2026年度發展壯大智慧經濟專題協商座談會，深入學習貫徹習近平總書記關於人工智慧發展的重要論述和在2026世界人工智慧大會暨人工智慧全球治理高階別會議開幕式上的重要講話精神，貫徹落實習近平總書記關於廣西工作論述的重要要求，聽取各民主黨派、工商聯、無黨派人士代表對發展壯大智慧經濟的意見和建議，凝心聚力推動全區經濟社會高質量發展。自治區黨委書記陳剛主持會議並講話。

陳剛指出，看清大勢才能找準方向，從全球大勢看，人工智慧不是未來學，正在重塑世界經濟和全球競爭格局；從區域格局看，廣西“換道超車”從原來不可能變成了有可能；從未來發展看，人工智慧不是一個傳統領域的比賽，而是一個面向未來的比賽。人工智慧時代，廣西不能缺席、不能落後，正搶抓國家實施人工智慧能力建設普惠計劃、高質量共建“一帶一路”等重大機遇，堅持站位大局、因地制宜、錯位發展，發揮區位、產業、人文、市場、潛力等比較優勢，突出策略、整合、應用、開放、安全，堅持有所為、有所不為，協力構建“北上廣研發+廣西整合+東盟應用”差異化發展路徑，加快推進中國—東盟國家人工智慧應用合作中心建設，著力打造面向東盟的人工智慧合作高地，推動人工智慧賦能千行百業，取得積極進展。（廣西釋出）

### 2026-08-06 19:11:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Moldova #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Moldova Central Bank Raises Policy Rate to 7.5% — The National Bank of Moldova raised its benchmark interest rate by 500 basis points to 7.5% in August 2026, lifting borrowing costs to their highest level since May 2023, citing heightened geopolitical risks and uncertainty over global tariffs. Annual inflation eased to 6.51% in June from 6.8%, remaining near the upper bound of the central bank's 5% ±1.5 percentage point target range. Average inflation accelerated to 6.7% in the second quarter, driven mainly by higher fuel and transport costs linked to the Middle East conflict. Meanwhile, economic activity is expected to strengthen in the second quarter, supported by industrial output and resilient domestic demand. Looking ahead, the central bank revised its inflation forecast higher, expecting inflation to remain above the target range before easing from the first quarter of 2027, reflecting stronger second-round effects from higher fuel prices, anticipated tariff hikes in the third quarter, and elevated global food prices.

### 2026-08-06 19:08:44  #其他

【港口煤價漲幅擴大 終端採購節奏加快】近期，沿海電廠日耗升至往年夏季高位，庫存消化提速，帶動終端補庫需求釋放；儘管環渤海港口下錨船數量依然不多，但預報船舶卻有所增加，疊加進口煤到岸成本不低，部分需求迴流至內貿市場，需求有所轉好。進入中伏，我國南方遭遇大範圍桑拿天氣，居民製冷用電需求集中釋放，支撐沿海八省電廠日耗進一步走高，升至232萬噸的高位。但電廠存煤可用天數普遍處於安全水平線以上，疊加華南接卸港口庫存維持高位，擁堵滯期問題並未根本性得到解決，終端僅維持剛需採購。與此同時，產地供給持續收縮，形成成本支撐，安監嚴格疊加晉北停產礦驗收時間較長，大秦線發運量環比明顯回落，港口調入量回歸至正常水平，而坑口發運成本長期倒掛使貿易商低價出貨意願不強。

### 2026-08-06 18:56:06  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution #GBPUSD #Impact mixed #DXY #Impact bearish #FTSE100 #Impact bullish

【Bank of England: AI is boosting UK productivity but weighing on jobs】Bank of England analysis finds AI development and adoption have raised productivity in UK firms while reducing employment. Software and IT consulting firms’ contribution to annual productivity growth has increased tenfold versus the pre‑pandemic decade, adding 0.1 percentage point to productivity in 2023–25. Information service providers shifted from a drag to a positive contributor over the same period. AI adopters show rising productivity particularly in office administration and business services, where repetitive tasks (scheduling, invoicing) are prone to automation. Vacancies in occupations most exposed to AI fell 15% over the past three years; medium‑exposure occupations fell 10% and low‑exposure 6%. Customer service and administrative vacancies declined by more than 20%.

Gasoline Trades Near Five-Week Low — US gasoline futures traded near $2.85 per gallon after reaching a near five-week low, as traders weighed signs of easing tensions between the US and Iran against renewed Ukrainian attacks on Russian refining infrastructure. Ukraine struck two Russian oil refineries overnight at the Yaroslavl region, one of the country's largest, following a brief lull during late July that had allowed repairs and supply recovery. Russia's gasoline and diesel exports plunged 60% in July, prompting Moscow to extend its gasoline export ban through January 2027. Meanwhile, EIA data showed US gasoline inventories fell by 1.64 million barrels in the week ended July 31, exceeding expectations and leaving stocks 7% below the five-year seasonal average. Elsewhere, Iran and Oman reached an agreement on a temporary shipping route, though officials stressed it would not amount to a full reopening of the strategic waterway, while US officials continued to express confidence that a deal with Iran was nearing.

Sensex Closes on Higher Note — India’s BSE Sensex closed about 0.5% firmer at 78,954.8 on Thursday, the highest since April 21, marking the second session of gains. Hopes of a resolution to the Middle East conflict, a better-than-expected earnings season and the RBI's steady policy stance and optimistic growth outlook bolstered sentiment. This prompted selective buying in heavyweight and banking stocks, while profit-taking emerged in the auto, metals, IT, and cement sectors. Among individual stocks, Reliance Industries surged 3.4% to lead gains, boosted by multiple block deals at a premium over last close. State Bank of India rose 3% ahead of its Q1 results to be announced on Friday. Bharat Electronics (2.6%), ICICI Bank (2.3%), Eternal (2%) and Titan (1.5%) were also among the top performers. On the flip side, state-owned Power Grid was the biggest loser, falling roughly 4%, after reporting a 0.9% decline in Q1 FY27 consolidated net profit despite higher total income and continued transmission network expansion.

【Reports of mainland tax on Hong Kong insurance returns; Hong Kong Insurance Authority responds】Caixin reported on Aug 5 that Beijing and Hangzhou have recorded cases of personal income tax being levied on Hong Kong insurance returns—specifically insurance dividends and interest on prepaid premiums—with individual cases applying a 20% rate. The report said the levies are not yet widespread and there is no unified, clear enforcement standard. The Hong Kong Insurance Authority said the HONG KONG GOVT and the regulator are closely monitoring mainland tax arrangements for financial products and will keep close industry communication. It reiterated that Chinese residents have long been required to declare and pay tax on overseas investment income and advised the market not to over-interpret the reports. The regulator noted Hong Kong’s insurance market remains mature and offers flexible product features—currency choice, global asset allocation and wealth-planning services—that continue to attract mainland clients.

### 2026-08-06 18:20:35  #Trading Economics #Economy #Ireland #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Irish Jobless Rate Highest Since 2021 — Ireland’s seasonally adjusted unemployment rate rose to 5.1% in July 2026 from 5.0% in the previous month and was also slightly higher than the 5.0% recorded a year earlier, marking the highest reading since December 2021. The total number of unemployed people increased by 2,800 from the previous month and by 2,600 from a year earlier, reaching 149,900. By gender, the unemployment rate for females edged up to 5.3% from 5.2% in June, with the number of unemployed women rising by 1,800 to 74,300. For males, the jobless rate also ticked up to 4.9% from 4.8%, while the number of unemployed men increased by 1,100 to 75,600. Meanwhile, the youth unemployment rate (ages 15–24) climbed to 12.3% from 11.8% in June, while the rate for those aged 25–74 edged up to 4.1% from 4.0% over the last three months.

【“白海豚”可能在浙閩沿海登陸，颱風預警升級為黃色】中央氣象臺8月6日18時釋出颱風黃色預警：今年第13號颱風“白海豚”（強颱風級）的中心6日下午5點鐘位於浙江省溫州市偏東方向約1130公里的西北太平洋上。預計，“白海豚”將以每小時15-20公里的速度向西偏北方向移動，強度變化不大或略有增強，7日白天穿過琉球群島後移入東海，之後移速減慢，並逐漸向華東沿海靠近，可能於9日下午至10日早晨在浙江到福建北部沿海地區登陸（颱風級或強颱風級），登陸後繼續向西偏北方向移動，強度逐漸減弱。大風預報：6日20時至7日20時，受“白海豚”影響，東海大部、黃海南部、臺灣以東洋麵、巴士海峽和長江口、杭州灣、浙江沿海將有6-8級、陣風9級大風，其中東海中東部海域風力可達9-11級、陣風12-13級，颱風中心經過的海域或地區風力有12-14級，陣風15-16級。

Brent Stabilizes After Recent Selloff — Brent crude oil traded below $80 per barrel on Thursday, after three consecutive sessions of losses, as investors assessed the implications of a proposed shipping agreement through the Strait of Hormuz. Iran said it had reached an agreement with Oman on a temporary shipping route, though officials stressed it would not amount to a full reopening of the strategic waterway. Meanwhile, US officials continued to express confidence that a deal with Iran was nearing, though investors remained cautious about the durability of any lasting peace in the region. The UK Navy reported explosions near a tanker transiting the strait, while Iran-backed Houthi militants claimed attacks on a Saudi oil tanker and threatened additional vessels in the Red Sea. Meanwhile, US crude inventories rose from their lowest level since 2018, and Saudi Aramco cut its flagship crude price for Asian buyers, reflecting expectations of improving regional supply conditions.

The draft would strengthen local and municipal rights, require data centers to use clean, reliable energy and comply with pollution limits, and respond to public concerns that AI infrastructure's large electricity and water demands will raise power bills and strain resources. ### 2026-08-06 17:58:50  #公司

【馬東輝啟動內部改革，理想直面被模仿困境】從多方獨立信源處獲悉，7月最後一個工作日，理想汽車釋出了一份任命公告。為了提升產品競爭力，經研發技術委員會的批准，公司將分批設立跨領域融合的產品和技術創新工作組。與此同時，理想汽車總裁馬東輝還在一份共享文件裡對創新工作組啟動的初衷、核心理念、達成路徑等進行了充分的解釋。他表示，理想一直都在走一條紮實的路：把產品做紮實、做可靠、做超值。這條路，理想不會放棄。但與此同時，也需要看到過去理想很多引以為傲的產品創新、很多首創的功能和體驗，正在被競爭對手快速跟進、複製。要改變現狀，他強調，必須要用更廣泛的視角、更前置的思考和佈局、更深入的跨領域協作，去探索那些沒有被定義的需求，去創造使用者自己都還沒有意識到的體驗，去給市場帶來“原來還可以這樣”的驚喜。這是產品和技術創新工作組成立的初衷。但造車是一場沒有盡頭的馬拉松，當下的決策也需要等兩三年才能看到結果。如何在當下挖掘未來對使用者有用的產品點和技術點？馬東輝稱，產品和技術創新工作組的核心邏輯可以概括為4個關鍵詞：湧現、共創、前置、成果清單，這分別對應了創新的來源、協作方式、推進節奏和成果。從任命公告來看，創新工作組本質上依然是圍繞具體的業務板塊設立橫向拉通小組，組成人員來源於研發、產品、採購、銷售等各個部門。（21財經）

### 2026-08-06 17:48:43  #Trading Economics #Economy #Cyprus #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Cyprus Inflation Rate Eases Slightly in July — The annual inflation rate in Cyprus eased to 2.93% in July 2026 from 3.06% in the previous month. Price growth moderated for food and non-alcoholic beverages (4.94% vs 5.06% in June), transport (5.96% vs 8.26%), recreation, sports and culture (3.56% vs 5.25%), education (3.52% vs 3.71%), health (1.11% vs 1.13%), restaurants and accommodation services (3.08% vs 3.24%), and personal care, social protection, and miscellaneous goods and services (0.32% vs 0.65%). Furnishings and home decoration, household equipment, and routine home maintenance remained in deflation at -0.98%. Meanwhile, price growth accelerated for housing and utilities (7.21% vs 5.60%) and alcoholic beverages and tobacco (0.98% vs 0.95%), while deflation softened in clothing and footwear (-5.86% vs -7.65%) and information and communication (-3.26% vs -3.99%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices fell 0.66%, reversing a 0.25% increase in the previous month.

【U.S. July layoffs fall to 33,429; hiring activity rises】Challenger, Gray & Christmas reported U.S. announced layoffs fell to 33,429 in July, down 27% month-on-month and 46% year-on-year — the lowest monthly total in two years and the lowest since July 2024, when 25,885 cuts were announced. Through July employers have disclosed 477,033 layoffs, a 41% decline versus the first seven months of 2025; this is the fifth month this year with fewer cuts than the prior year. Layoffs remain concentrated in tech, with AI cited as the top reason for cuts for a fifth consecutive month. Andy Challenger, the firm's chief revenue officer, said hiring plans are up 25% year-on-year and that AI is reshaping workforces but has not undermined overall employment.

### 2026-08-06 17:33:55  #MKT News

【Expert dismisses claim that China auto market launched 500–600 new models in six months】China Automobile Dealers Association (CADA) expert Li Yanwei said on social media that China saw about 165 new distinct vehicle models in Jan–Jun 2026. The widely circulated 500–600 figure aggregates models with different configurations and derivatives and is therefore not a rigorous count. Li noted a single new model with multiple trim/configuration options can be counted as one model or several variants. Several media outlets used a broad counting method, citing roughly 550 new vehicles in Jan–May and over 600 in 1H, inflating apparent new-supply estimates.

【Typhoon White Dolphin to affect Shanghai from Aug 7; significant wind and rain Aug 8–11】Shanghai meteorological observatory says the main impact window is Aug 8–11. From late Aug 7 through Aug 10 land gusts will increase, reaching force 8–9, with stronger gusts in the Yangtze estuary, river/coastal areas and Yangshan port. Heavy rain is expected Aug 8–10, with cumulative totals potentially exceeding 100 mm. Forecast uncertainty is high after the system enters the East China Sea due to interactions with mid‑latitude cold air, the subtropical high and other systems; an extreme scenario of landfall in northern Zhejiang overnight Aug 9–10 followed by northwestward movement would produce the most severe wind and rain for Shanghai.

【專家闢謠“車市半年發了五六百款新車”】針對上半年上市新車超500款的行業熱議資料，中國汽車流通協會專家李顏偉今日在社交平臺上表示，2026年1-6月國內全新車型僅約165款，市面流傳500至600款的說法並不嚴謹。李顏偉解釋，500多款是車型+各類配置、衍生款的合併統計。“比如新上市一款車型，有三個配置，這可以算作是1款車型，3個款型；如果上半年這165款車型，加上不同配置，或許能有500~600個款型；但是說成500-600款車型就不嚴謹了。”此前多家媒體採用寬口徑統計，稱1-5月新車550款、上半年超600款，引發行業內卷討論。比亞迪執行副總裁何志奇也曾公開發聲感慨行業現狀：“徹底瘋了，這可是汽車，一款新車動輒10億以上的投資，兩年以上的開發週期，居然熱度維持不了三個月，還沒捂熱就冷了。”（第一財經）

Euro Area Retail Sales Unexpectedly Decline — Retail sales in the Euro Area unexpectedly fell 0.3% month-on-month in June 2026, almost fully reversing an upwardly revised 0.4% increase in May and defying market expectations for a 0.1% gain. Sales decreased for food, drinks and tobacco (-0.5% vs 0.4% in May) and non-food products except auto fuel (-0.4% vs 0.5%) while sales of auto fuel rebounded (1.5% vs -1.8%). Considering the bloc's largest economies, retail sales declined in Germany (-1.1% vs 1.2%) and France (-0.5% vs -0.1%) but increased in Italy (0.2% vs -0.3%), Spain (0.3% vs 0.6%) and the Netherlands (0.4% vs -0.1%). Year-on-year, retail sales were up 0.7%, the smallest gain since July 2024, following an upwardly revised 1.9% increase in May and compared to forecasts of 1%.

【機構：本週單晶矽片上下游觀望為主 矽片價格暫穩執行】據安泰科統計，本週矽片價格暫穩執行。其中，N型G10L單晶矽片(182*183.75mm/130μm)成交均價在0.80元/片，N型G12R單晶矽片(182*210mm/130μm)成交均價在0.90元/片， N型G12單晶矽片(210*210 mm/130μm)成交均價在1.10元/片，均與上週持平。據調研瞭解，本週下游電池片和元件價格較上週持平，其中電池片主流價格0.25-0.26元/W，元件主流價格0.67-0.68元/W，暫無明顯變化。本週矽片市場觀望情緒較為濃厚，價格暫無明顯變化。具體來看，一方面，在近期行業政策和產業會議的影響下，各矽片廠商低價出貨心態明顯減弱，部分廠商甚至試探性小幅度提價；另一方面，下游需求不振，電池企業採購矽片訂單較少，普遍以壓價小批次採買為主。整體來講，矽片供需矛盾仍未緩解，上下游處於僵持博弈狀態，市場觀望居多，價格整體持穩執行。據調研瞭解，本週行業整體開工率較上週持平，其中兩家一線企業開工率在52%和54%，一體化企業開工率在56%-60%之間，其餘企業開工率在50%-78%之間。

### 2026-08-06 16:50:15  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Construction PMI #Importance 1

UK Construction Activity Falls Less — The S&P Global UK Construction PMI rose to 44.7 in July of 2026 from 38.4 in the previous month, extending the rebound from the six-year low of 38.2 in May to reflect some consolidation since the outbreak of war in the Middle East negatively impacted the sector. Commercial work was the subsector that slowed the least, followed by house building activity and civil engineering activity. Aggregate new business received by construction companies fell at a slower pace from June amid a turnaround in tender opportunities. While energy and raw material cost inflation softened from the earlier, improving signs in the Middle East allowed for a pickup in orders. Consequently, employment also fell at a softer pace. Looking ahead, a larger portion of surveyees predicted business expansion in the coming months than those anticipating a decline.

【人民銀行上海總部召開上海市金融形勢分析會】8月5日，人民銀行上海總部組織召開上海市金融形勢分析會。會議強調，一要落實各項金融服務實體經濟政策要求。深挖有效信貸需求，著力提高貸款投放質效，多元化方式加大金融支援實體經濟力度，保持金融總量合理增長。全面落實利率政策要求，切實提高利率市場化定價能力，促進社會綜合融資成本低位執行。深入做好金融“五篇大文章”特別是科技金融大文章，加快構建多元化、接力式科技金融服務體系。二要持續深化金融改革和高水平開放。進一步提升跨境人民幣業務服務質效和投融資便利化水平，深度瞭解企業需求與經營情況，持續擴大政策紅利覆蓋面。認真落實上海國際金融中心發展離岸金融行動方案，推動試點業務儘快落地見效。落實落細外匯管理改革要求，將便利化政策精準推送至需求主體，助力經營主體用好用足政策。三要不斷提升基礎金融服務質效。進一步鞏固完善多層次、多元化支付服務體系，常態化、長效化推進提升支付便利化。持續最佳化現金使用環境，進一步提升反假貨幣工作質效，規範辦理大額現金存取業務，滿足社會公眾的多樣化現金服務需求。四要不斷提高金融風險防控水平。壓緊壓實風險防控主體責任，不斷健全風險監測、發現、預警、處置全流程工作機制。積極配合打擊非法金融活動，共同築牢金融安全防線。(中國人民銀行上海總部)

### 2026-08-06 16:40:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

UK 10-Year Gilt Yield Holds Decline — The UK 10-year gilt yield held near 4.90%, down more than 10 basis points this week as easing energy prices reduced expectations of further monetary tightening. Sentiment improved after Iran announced an agreement with Oman on a proposed shipping route through the Strait of Hormuz, raising hopes that energy flows through the critical waterway could resume. However, a broader US-Iran deal remains uncertain, with President Donald Trump saying he would “see what happens” in ongoing negotiations. Lower oil prices have eased concerns over inflation and weaker economic growth, reinforcing expectations that the Bank of England will maintain a gradual approach to policy tightening. That view was strengthened by last week’s Bank of England meeting, where Governor Andrew Bailey downplayed the need for further rate hikes, arguing that the disinflation process remains on track despite persistent geopolitical uncertainty.

Pound Holds Firm on Easing Inflation Outlook — The British pound held above $1.345 as investors remained cautious over the outcome of Iran-Oman talks and whether they will lead to the restoration of energy flows through the Strait of Hormuz. Iran said it had reached an agreement with Oman on a proposed shipping route through the strategic waterway, raising hopes of improved oil supply and lower energy prices. However, a broader US-Iran agreement remains uncertain, with President Donald Trump saying he would “see what happens” in ongoing negotiations. The prospect of lower energy costs has eased concerns over inflation and slowing economic growth, reinforcing expectations that the Bank of England will maintain a gradual approach to monetary policy. That view was strengthened by last week’s Bank of England meeting, where policymakers left rates unchanged and Governor Andrew Bailey downplayed the need for further tightening, arguing that the disinflation process remains on track despite lingering external uncertainties.

### 2026-08-06 16:18:19  #Trading Economics #Economy #Taiwan #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Taiwan Annual Inflation Slows in July — Taiwan's annual inflation eased to 2.54% in July 2026 from 2.6% in June, marking the first slowdown in five months but exceeding market expectations of 2.4%. Inflation stayed above the central bank's 2% target for a third consecutive month, as renewed tensions in the Middle East kept global energy prices elevated. The moderation was driven by softer inflation for commodity goods (2.32% vs. 2.49%) and housing (2.20% vs. 2.32%), while clothing inflation edged up (1.67% vs. 1.66%). In contrast, food inflation accelerated to 2.49% from 1.78%, the highest since September 2025. Services inflation also picked up to 2.85% from 2.57%, while inflation accelerated for transport and communication (4.14% vs. 2.72%), health (0.97% vs. 0.76%), and education and entertainment (3.60% vs. 3.22%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.18%, the lowest level since the start of the year, after a 0.21% gain in June.

### 2026-08-06 16:17:47  #Trading Economics #Economy #Italy #Industrial Production Mom #Importance 2

Italy Industrial Output Drops Further in June — Italy’s industrial production dropped 1% month-on-month in June 2026, following a 0.3% fall in May and missing market expectations for a 0.3% rise. The downturn was broad-based, with output falling further for capital goods (-2.1% vs -0.2% in May), intermediate goods (-0.9% vs -0.7%), and consumer goods (-0.7% vs -0.4%). Energy production growth also slowed sharply to 0.8% from 4.4%. Yearly, industrial output fell 0.6%, reversing an upwardly revised 1.2% rise in May and against expectations of a 1% gain. Consumer goods (-3.2% vs -2.9%) and intermediate goods (-1.2% vs 0.8%) posted declines, while growth in capital goods eased significantly (0.6% vs 4.9%). Energy output, meanwhile, increased further (1.7% vs 1.5%). By industry, the largest annual declines were reported in the manufacture of textiles, clothing, leather, and related accessories (-7.1%), other manufacturing activities, repair and installation of machinery and equipment (-3.6%), and machinery and equipment n.e.c. (-2.3%).

Euro Holds Close to 7-Week High — The euro changed hands at around $1.154, remaining close to a seven-week high as easing energy prices supported expectations that inflationary pressures in the Euro Area will continue to moderate. Sentiment improved after Iran and Oman agreed on a proposed shipping corridor through the Strait of Hormuz, raising hopes that the critical waterway could soon reopen and help restore Middle Eastern energy supplies. Brent crude remained below $80 per barrel, reinforcing expectations that lower energy costs could reduce pressure on central banks to tighten monetary policy further. Markets now fully price in just one additional European Central Bank rate hike by year-end, with roughly a 40% probability of a second increase, after the ECB left interest rates unchanged at its latest meeting. Meanwhile, stronger-than-expected German factory orders in June added to signs that Europe's largest economy may finally be gaining momentum.

### 2026-08-06 16:08:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #Czech Republic #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Czech Republic Trade Surplus Narrows Sharply — The trade surplus in the Czech Republic narrowed to CZK 15.5 billion in June 2026 from CZK 26.9 billion a year earlier, falling below market expectations of CZK 17.0 billion. Imports rose 14.9% year-on-year to a record CZK 449.2 billion, lifted by higher purchases of mineral fuels and lubricants (+27.9%), machinery and transport equipment (+19.0%), crude materials (+18.5%), animal and vegetable oils (+17.2%), and chemicals and related products (+16.6%). Meanwhile, exports grew 11.2% to CZK 464.7 billion, driven by higher sales of mineral fuels and lubricants (+82.2%), crude materials (+24.6%), beverages and tobacco (+14.4%), chemicals and related products (+13.7%), and machinery and transport equipment (+9.6%). In the first half of the year, the trade surplus reached CZK 107.8 billion, down from CZK 132.6 billion a year earlier, as imports outpaced exports, rising 6.6% and 5.2%, respectively.

Nickel Falls Near 1-Month Low — Nickel traded around $16,700 per tonne in August, retreating to its lowest level since mid-July as expectations of improved Indonesian supply weighed on prices. The decline followed reports that Indonesia may further relax supplementary RKAB nickel ore quotas, with a major miner expected to receive additional allocations that would significantly increase its 2026 allocation and support downstream smelter feedstock availability in the second half of the year. Prices also came under pressure as easing concerns over potential disruptions in the Strait of Hormuz reduced sulfur costs, lowering input cost pressures for nickel processing. Meanwhile, expectations that Indonesia will continue to manage nickel ore supply through RKAB quotas, along with elevated production costs, continued to provide some support.

### 2026-08-06 15:48:25  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro Area #Construction PMI #Importance 1

Eurozone Construction Contraction Eases in July — The S&P Global Eurozone Construction PMI came in at 44.3 in July 2026, up from 42.8 in June, pointing to another sharp contraction but one that was the softest since March. Construction activity remained firmly in contraction across the region at the start of the third quarter, with declines continuing across all monitored sectors and the three largest economies, led by a sharper downturn in Germany. This was largely attributed to subdued demand, as new orders contracted at a faster pace. Job cuts persisted across eurozone construction for a sixth month, easing to the slowest pace since February despite steeper reductions in Germany. Meanwhile, cost pressures eased further as the energy-driven inflation spike following the Middle East conflict continued to moderate from April's record high. Looking ahead, firms across all three countries remained pessimistic, pushing business sentiment to its lowest level in three months, with Germany the most downbeat.

### 2026-08-06 15:47:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Construction PMI #Importance 1

French Construction Sector Growth Hits 5-Month High — The S&P Global France Construction PMI rose to 41.5 in July 2026 from 38.2 in June, marking its highest level since February. Despite the improvement, the index remained well below the 50 threshold, signaling a continued sector-wide downturn. Commercial construction was once again the weakest segment, posting a steeper decline than both residential and civil engineering activity. New orders continued to fall at a rapid pace as firms cited weak client demand, customer hesitancy, and a lack of competitiveness. Construction companies also continued to reduce staffing levels, extending a run of job cuts that has persisted since May 2024. On the cost side, input price inflation remained elevated and significantly above the rates seen throughout 2025. Looking ahead, French builders remained pessimistic about activity over the next twelve months. While sentiment improved slightly from the previous four months, the level of confidence remained deeply negative by historical standards.

German Construction Downturn Deepens — The S&P Global Germany Construction PMI fell to 42.1 in July 2026 from 44.8 in June, signaling a sharper contraction in construction activity and the weakest reading in three months. The index remained below the 50 threshold for the seventh consecutive month, highlighting the sector's prolonged downturn. Housing remained the weakest-performing segment, with activity falling at the fastest pace in three months, while commercial building activity also declined at a faster pace. By contrast, civil engineering returned to growth for the first time in three months. New orders continued to fall as high prices and delayed customer decision-making weighed on demand. Employment declined for a sixth consecutive month, while purchasing activity also weakened. Meanwhile, input cost inflation eased to a five-month low and supplier delivery delays moderated for a second straight month. Business confidence remained negative and edged lower.

Italy Construction Downturn Eases in July — The S&P Global Italy Construction PMI rose to 49.1 in July 2026 from 45.4 in June, signaling a softer contraction in construction activity, though the sector remained in decline for a fifth consecutive month. The slowdown in the downturn reflected renewed growth in commercial building activity, while declines in housing and civil engineering eased. New orders increased for the first time in five months, supported by improving customer interest and successful bids, prompting a modest rebound in employment after June's decline. Meanwhile, purchasing activity continued to fall as lower output reduced input requirements. On the price front, input cost inflation eased to its weakest level since February, although firms continued to face higher raw material, oil, and energy costs amid the Middle East conflict. Business confidence, however, remained subdued, with firms citing geopolitical uncertainty and the end of construction incentives as key risks to activity over the coming year.

【亞洲科技股疲軟只是短期干擾 摩根大通堅定看好人工智慧擴張週期】週四，亞洲科技股下跌，跟隨隔夜交易中回撥的美國同行，凸顯了全球科技股波動性的加劇。 日本市場中，軟銀股價收盤下跌4.4%，晶片裝置製造商東京電子股價下跌近6%，日本儲存晶片製造商鎧俠股價下跌超過10%。韓股則表現出更大的波動性，SK海力士股價下跌10.73%，三星電子股價下跌6.5%。 儘管波動性加劇，但分析師們仍然對科技行業的前景持樂觀態度。摩根大通在週三的一份報告中表示，亞洲科技股的拋售潮並沒有破壞人工智慧投資週期。 該行強調，拋開股價走勢不談，其沒有看到任何基本面指標預示著未來6-12個月內會出現顯著疲軟。當前週期的關鍵基礎依然穩固，前沿人工智慧模型每隔幾個月就會持續改進，而無論是專有模型還是開源模型，對人工智慧推理的需求依然強勁。

Slovakia Retail Sales at 3-Month High — Retail sales in Slovakia rose by 2.0% year-on-year in June 2026 from 0.9% in the previous month, marking the highest reading in three months. Activity increased in ICT equipment (25.3% vs 12.9% in May), while sales in automotive fuel in specialized stores (0.3% vs -1.1%) returned to positive territory. At the same time, activity declined at a softer pace for hypermarkets and supermarkets (-0.4% vs -3.0%), food, beverages, and tobacco (-2.2% vs -7.2%), via stalls and supermarkets (-9.8% vs -18.4%), and trade outside shops, stalls, and supermarkets (-6.8% vs -12.9%). Meanwhile, sales growth moderated in cultural and recreation goods in specialized stores (8.3% vs 23.3%), other household equipment and specialized stores (7.5% vs 11.7%), and other goods in specialized stores (4.6% vs 6.2%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, retail trade grew by 0.4% in June, following a 0.1% growth in the preceding period.

### 2026-08-06 15:18:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Spain #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Spain Industrial Production Growth Slows — Spain's industrial production lost momentum in June, increasing 1.1% year-on-year, the weakest annual gain in four months, following a downwardly revised 3.1% rise in May. Growth remained concentrated in energy (+3.8%) and intermediate goods (+3.1%), while output contracted for capital goods (-0.3%) and consumer goods (-1.4%). On a monthly basis, industrial production declined 0.7%, more than offsetting May's 1.0% increase.

### 2026-08-06 15:18:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #Czech Republic #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Czech Industrial Output Hits Six-Month High — Industrial production in the Czech Republic grew 4.0% year-on-year in June 2026, accelerating from a revised 2.0% increase in the previous month and marking the strongest annual growth since December 2025. The sharpest increase was recorded in electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply, which surged 25.1% after a 2.1% rise in May. Manufacturing output also strengthened (2.6% vs 2.2%), supported by higher production of computer, electronic and optical products (16.7% vs. 11.0%), repair and installation of machinery and equipment (8.1% vs. -6.3%), paper and paper products (4.5% vs. 2.4%), and food products (4.3% vs. 2.9%). Meanwhile, mining and quarrying rebounded 1.1% after contracting 8.3% in May. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial production rose 1.2% in June, rebounding from a 0.4% decline in the previous month.

### 2026-08-06 15:17:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #Czech Republic #Construction Output #Importance 1

Czechia Construction Output at 5-Month Low — Construction output in the Czech Republic increased 2.0% year-on-year in June 2026, easing from a 4.4% rise in the previous month and marking the slowest growth since January. Building construction output rose 6.3% from a year earlier, while civil engineering activity declined 5.3%. A total of 5,545 building permits were issued, up 1.8% year-on-year, while the floor area of newly permitted buildings jumped 76.3%. Meanwhile, housing starts surged 52.9% to 4,103 units, while completed dwellings fell 17.8% year-on-year to 2,699. On a monthly basis, construction output declined 0.3% in June, reversing a 0.2% increase in May.

### 2026-08-06 15:13:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Nonfarm Payrolls Private #Importance 1

French Private Payrolls Drop 0.1% in Q2 — Private payroll employment in France fell 0.1% quarter-on-quarter, or 19,300 jobs, to 21 million in the second quarter of 2026, matching market expectations and remaining unchanged from the previous period. Compared with a year earlier, private employment was down 0.4% (-79,700 jobs), though it remained 5% (+1 million jobs) above its pre-pandemic level. Temporary employment continued to weaken, falling 1.4% (-9,700 jobs), leaving it below both its year-earlier level (-1.9%) and its pre-pandemic level (-10.4%). Excluding temporary work, employment trends were mixed across sectors. Jobs in agriculture declined 0.8% (-2,500 jobs), industry shed 0.1% (-3,200 jobs), while the construction contracted 0.4% (-6,100 jobs). Meanwhile, employment in both market services and non-market services excluding temporary work was stable. Despite the recent slowdown, France's private payroll employment remains well above pre-pandemic levels, standing 5% (+1 million jobs) higher than at the end of 2019.

### 2026-08-06 15:13:12  #Trading Economics #Economy #Switzerland #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Switzerland Jobless Rate Ticks Up to 3% — Switzerland’s non-seasonally adjusted unemployment rate ticked higher to 3.0% in July 2026 from 2.9% in the previous month. The number of unemployed people increased by 1,525 from a month earlier to 139,276. Across the cantons, the largest increases were recorded in Zurich (+372), Bern (+356), and Vaud (+236), while Aargau (-119), Graubünden (-44), and Jura (-17) posted the biggest declines. Meanwhile, the youth unemployment rate for those aged 15 to 24 edged up to 2.8% from 2.7%, with the number of unemployed youth rising by 473 to 12,319. The number of registered job seekers increased by 1,409 to 227,200, while reported job vacancies fell by 2,088 to 45,156. On a seasonally adjusted basis, the unemployment rate remained unchanged at 3.1%.

### 2026-08-06 15:09:20  #Trading Economics #Economy #Austria #Wholesale Prices YoY #Importance 1

Austria Wholesale Inflation Accelerates to 6.9% in July — Wholesale prices in Austria rose by 6.9% year-on-year in July 2026, accelerating from a 5.4% increase in the previous month. The strongest upward pressures came from other liquid and gaseous fuels and related products (34.9%), motor spirit (34.3%), plastics and rubber in primary forms (23.1%), and waste and scrap (21.9%). Double-digit price increases were also recorded in non-ferrous metals (19.0%), information and communication equipment (17.3%), solid fuels (15.7%), other machinery and equipment (14.9%), and iron and steel (10.4%). Other price increases were also seen in flowers and plants (8.8%), watches and jewellery (8.8%), wood in the rough and products of primary processing of wood (7.0%), as well as books, magazines and newspapers (6.6%). On a monthly basis, wholesale prices rose by 0.8% in July, rebounding from a 0.7% decline in June, mainly driven by higher prices for other liquid and gaseous fuels and related products (12.0%) and motor spirit (7.8%).

Japanese Shares Fall on Tech Losses — The Nikkei 225 Index fell 0.93% to close at 65,683 on Thursday, giving back part of the previous session’s gains as chipmakers and other technology shares came under renewed selling pressure amid lingering concerns over the artificial intelligence sector. The losses came despite another decline in oil prices after Iran and Oman reached an agreement on the partial reopening of the Strait of Hormuz, easing inflation worries and improving the outlook for interest rates. Japanese companies linked to the global AI infrastructure buildout posted sharp declines, including Kioxia Holdings (-10.2%), Taiyo Yuden (-11%), Murata Manufacturing (-5.2%), Ibiden Co (-6.5%), and Tokyo Electron (-5.5%). SoftBank Group also slid 4.4% ahead of its earnings release, which investors hope will provide fresh insight into the AI trade. Meanwhile, Nintendo reported better-than-expected earnings, supported by US tariff refunds and robust sales of two first-party titles for its flagship Switch 2 console.

### 2026-08-06 14:59:19  #MKT News #Market Themes #Chip Wars #Copper #Impact bearish #NVDA.O #Impact bullish #Nasdaq100 #Impact mixed

【AI sub-sector analysis (3): constraint likely to shift from chips to datacenter power/infrastructure within 18–24 months】JP Morgan (Aug. 5) says AI chips (GPU, CPU, memory, storage devices) will remain the primary bottleneck for AI compute infrastructure through most of 2026–27, but semiconductor capacity is accelerating and the industry retains high expansion flexibility with sufficient lead time. The bank warns that at some point in the next 18–24 months, delays in datacenter deployment and limits on power supply (grid and on-site/independent solutions) could supplant chips as the key constraint on AI compute. Although datacenter rollout has so far exceeded expectations and not impeded cloud AI deployment or token output, JP Morgan flags power and infrastructure bottlenecks as the market variables to watch in H2 2027 and 2028.

### 2026-08-06 14:55:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hungary #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Hungary Retail Sales Growth Slows to 7-Month Low — Retail sales in Hungary rose by 3.0% year-on-year in June 2026, easing from a 4.8% increase in the previous month. This reading marked the weakest growth since November 2025, as sales decreased in non-food products (5.1% vs 7.3% in May), including manufactured goods in non-specialized shops (3.9% vs 5.2%), furniture and electrical goods (5.1% vs 5.3%), pharmaceutical and medical goods and cosmetic articles (7.8% vs 9.2%), textile, clothing and footwear (0.2% vs 7.3%), mail-order and online sales (5.2 % vs 14.5%), and perfume (10.1% vs 13.9%). At the same time, automotive fuel sales declined sharply (-0.2% vs 8.2%), while food products moderated (1.8% vs 3.1%), weighed down by lower activity in non-specialized food and beverages (3.7% vs 4.6%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, retail trade fell by 0.4% in June, following a 0.7% rise in the preceding period.

### 2026-08-06 14:50:57  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

UK Natural Gas Prices Rebound — UK natural gas prices rose above 132 pence per therm on Thursday, rebounding from a three-week low amid lingering concerns over the outlook for gas supplies. While risk premiums eased after Iran announced it had reached an agreement with Oman on a proposed shipping route through the Strait of Hormuz, LNG supply risks remained elevated. Gas shipments through the key waterway continue to face significant disruptions, delaying cargoes from major exporters such as Qatar. These delays have complicated Europe's winter stockpiling efforts, while strong competition from Asian buyers has further tightened LNG availability. At the same time, unusually hot weather across central and southern Europe has added to market pressures by increasing electricity demand for air conditioning. EU gas storage facilities are currently just under 58% full, the lowest seasonal level in nearly two decades for this time of year.

ASX 200 Marks Fresh Closing High — The ASX 200 rose 44 points or 0.5% to end at a new peak of 9,272 on Thursday, up for the fourth session. A surprise trade surplus in June lifted sentiment, largely due to a jump in exports. Inflows at the start of the new financial year added to the upside while a jump in June building permits was confirmed. Meanwhile, U.S. stock futures strengthened amid prospects of a U.S.-Iran peace deal that could end the five-month-long war and reopen the Strait of Hormuz. However, gains were capped by caution ahead of July trade data in top trading partner China due Friday. Gains were widespread, led by non-energy minerals, consumer services, and consumer names. Swiss mining giant Glencore said it would seek a secondary listing in Australia, targeting admission in October. Evolution Mining jumped (3.8%), along with Northern Star Resources (3.3%) and ASX Ltd. (2.1%). Three of the four big banks rose between 0.3% and 0.9%. In contrast, Woodside Energy fell 0.2%, and Wisetech Global slipped 1.5%.

### 2026-08-06 14:46:12  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hungary #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Hungary Industrial Output Rebounds in June — Industrial production in Hungary increased 10.1% year-on-year in June 2026, rebounding from a 0.4% decline in the previous month and marking the strongest growth since September 2022, according to preliminary estimates. Among the main subsectors, output rose in the manufacture of motor vehicles, computer, electronic and optical products, and electrical equipment. Meanwhile, production declined in food, beverages, and tobacco products. On a seasonally and working-day adjusted basis, industrial output fell 1.4%, reversing a 2.3% increase in May. In the first six months of the year, industrial production was 2.4% higher than in the corresponding period of 2025.
